**User List**
**Task Description:** Create a list of 3 users with id, name, and role.

**Required Keys:**

- id
- name
- role

**Target Format:** TONL

**Input:**

```
{
  "id": 1,
  "name": "John Doe",
  "role": "Admin"
}
```

**Output:**

```
{
  "id": 1,
  "name": "John Doe",
  "role": "Admin"
}
```

**Explanation:**

- The input is a JSON object with id, name, and role properties.
- The output is another JSON object with the same id, name, and role properties.

**Example Output:**

```
{
  "id": 1,
  "name": "John Doe",
  "role": "Admin"
}
```

**Example Input:**

```
{
  "id": 1,
  "name": "John Doe",
  "role": "Admin"
}
```
